[ASSISTANT]
PASS — design6 gives ADP **2.53M** (area 4274, delay 593) — new best. Dropping the control FFs saved 9% area but the derived controls cost 30ps.

Now the best of both worlds: **one-hot row controls** (`d=+1,+2,−1,−2`) registered at load time (16 FF/PE), driving a pure AND-OR row generator where the complements of A come free from AIG inversions. This should cut the row-gen from ~290ps to ~120ps while staying short:
  -> create_file(content="// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to\n// logikbench `tpu`.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:\n//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 19 bits (exact: |product| <= 2^14 and\n//    every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so |sum| <= 2^17; the golden\n//    32-bit datapath never wraps, so sign extension reproduces it exactly).\n//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is); all\n//    datapath state is overwritten with defined values before the first\n//    c_valid pulse, and c_data is only checked while c_valid is high.\n//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a\n//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2\n//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column\n//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.\n//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The Booth digit of\n//    each weight pair is decoded at LOAD time into a one-hot row control\n//    {+1,+2,-1,-2} (registered), so the per-cycle row generator is a pure\n//    AND-OR of the registered activation and its complement:\n//      y_i = (c1 & A) | (c2 & 2A) | (cn1 & ~A) | (cn2 & ~2A)\n//    A negated digit's +1 correction is kv at position 2i.  Only 7 vectors\n//    (4 rows + kv + ps + pc) feed a 4-level 3:2 compressor.  All arithmetic\n//    is exact modulo 2^19, matching the golden.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // accumulator width\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input               en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   ps_n,       // partial-sum vector in from above\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   pc_n,       // partial-carry vector in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] ps_s,     // partial-sum vector out to below\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] pc_s      // partial-carry vector out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- Booth radix-4 digit decode of the weight (registered at load) --\n   // digit i: d_i = w[2i-1] + w[2i] - 2*w[2i+1]   (w[-1] = 0)\n   wire [3:0] bh = {w_north[7], w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1]};\n   wire [3:0] bm = {w_north[6], w_north[4], w_north[2], w_north[0]};\n   wire [3:0] bl = {w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [3:0] bc1  = ~bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = +1\n   wire [3:0] bc2  = ~bh & bm & bl;     // d = +2\n   wire [3:0] bcn1 =  bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = -1\n   wire [3:0] bcn2 =  bh & ~bm & ~bl;   // d = -2\n\n   reg [3:0] c1_q, c2_q, cn1_q, cn2_q;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w     <= w_north;\n         c1_q  <= bc1;\n         c2_q  <= bc2;\n         cn1_q <= bcn1;\n         cn2_q <= bcn2;\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         ps_s   <= L4a[ACCW-1:0];\n         pc_s   <= L4a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW];\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- row generation: y_i = d_i * A without its 4^i shift -----------\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a19  = {{(ACCW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a219 = {{(ACCW-DW-1){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west, 1'b0};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a19n = ~a19;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a219n = ~a219;\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y0 = (c1_q[0] & a19) | (c2_q[0] & a219)\n                      | (cn1_q[0] & a19n) | (cn2_q[0] & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y1 = (c1_q[1] & a19) | (c2_q[1] & a219)\n                      | (cn1_q[1] & a19n) | (cn2_q[1] & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y2 = (c1_q[2] & a19) | (c2_q[2] & a219)\n                      | (cn1_q[2] & a19n) | (cn2_q[2] & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y3 = (c1_q[3] & a19) | (c2_q[3] & a219)\n                      | (cn1_q[3] & a19n) | (cn2_q[3] & a219n);\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r0 = y0;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r1 = y1 << 2;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r2 = y2 << 4;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r3 = y3 << 6;\n\n   // constant corrections +1 for the negated rows, at positions 0,2,4,6\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] kv = {{(ACCW-7){1'b0}},\n                         cn1_q[3] | cn2_q[3], 1'b0,\n                         cn1_q[2] | cn2_q[2], 1'b0,\n                         cn1_q[1] | cn2_q[1], 1'b0,\n                         cn1_q[0] | cn2_q[0]};\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressors ----------------------------------------------\n   function [2*ACCW-1:0] fa3;\n      input [ACCW-1:0] a, b, c;\n      reg   [ACCW-1:0] s, ch;\n      begin\n         s    = a ^ b ^ c;\n         ch   = (a & b) | (a & c) | (b & c);\n         fa3  = {{ch[ACCW-2:0], 1'b0}, s};   // carry-out of MSB dropped\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // 7 -> 5 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 vectors\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1a = fa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1b = fa3(r3, kv, ps_n);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L2a = fa3(L1a[ACCW-1:0], L1a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[ACCW-1:0]);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L3a = fa3(L2a[ACCW-1:0], L2a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[2*ACCW-1:ACCW]);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L4a = fa3(L3a[ACCW-1:0], L3a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], pc_n);\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]     a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]     w_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar            i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west  (a_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_n    (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_n    (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east  (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_s    (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_s    (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACW = 19;         // internal accumulator width\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zero fill when a_valid low\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar          i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n       reg   [ACCW-1:0] s, ch;

[Step 14/60]
// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to
// logikbench `tpu`.
//
// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:
//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 19 bits (exact: |product| <= 2^14 and
//    every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so |sum| <= 2^17; the golden
//    32-bit datapath never wraps, so sign extension reproduces it exactly).
//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is); all
//    datapath state is overwritten with defined values before the first
//    c_valid pulse, and c_data is only checked while c_valid is high.
//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a
//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2
//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column
//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.
//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The Booth digit of
//    each weight pair is decoded at LOAD time into a one-hot row control
//    {+1,+2,-1,-2} (registered), so the per-cycle row generator is a pure
//    AND-OR of the registered activation and its complement:
//      y_i = (c1 & A) | (c2 & 2A) | (cn1 & ~A) | (cn2 & ~2A)
//    A negated digit's +1 correction is kv at position 2i.  Only 7 vectors
//    (4 rows + kv + ps + pc) feed a 4-level 3:2 compressor.  All arithmetic
//    is exact modulo 2^19, matching the golden.

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 19) // accumulator width
   (
    input               clk,
    input               ld,         // weight-load shift enable
    input               en,         // compute enable
    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,     // activation in from west
    input  [ACCW-1:0]   ps_n,       // partial-sum vector in from above
    input  [ACCW-1:0]   pc_n,       // partial-carry vector in from above
    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] ps_s,     // partial-sum vector out to below
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] pc_s      // partial-carry vector out to below
    );

   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // ---- Booth radix-4 digit decode of the weight (registered at load) --
   // digit i: d_i = w[2i-1] + w[2i] - 2*w[2i+1]   (w[-1] = 0)
   wire [3:0] bh = {w_north[7], w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1]};
   wire [3:0] bm = {w_north[6], w_north[4], w_north[2], w_north[0]};
   wire [3:0] bl = {w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1], 1'b0};

   wire [3:0] bc1  = ~bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = +1
   wire [3:0] bc2  = ~bh & bm & bl;     // d = +2
   wire [3:0] bcn1 =  bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = -1
   wire [3:0] bcn2 =  bh & ~bm & ~bl;   // d = -2

   reg [3:0] c1_q, c2_q, cn1_q, cn2_q;

endmodule


module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,
    parameter DW = 8,
    parameter ACCW = 19)
   (
    input               clk,
    input               ld,
    input               en,
    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,
    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,
    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot
    );

   wire [DW-1:0]     a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0]   ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [ACCW-1:0]   pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]     w_v  [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar            i, j;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge
         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};
         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};
         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];
         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column
         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];
      end
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row
         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col
            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe
              (.clk     (clk),
               .ld      (ld),
               .en      (en),
               .w_north (w_v[i][j]),
               .a_west  (a_h[i][j]),
               .ps_n    (ps_v[i][j]),
               .pc_n    (pc_v[i][j]),
               .w_south (w_v[i+1][j]),
               .a_east  (a_h[i][j+1]),
               .ps_s    (ps_v[i+1][j]),
               .pc_s    (pc_v[i+1][j]));
         end
      end
   endgenerate

endmodule

    input               clk,
    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream
    input               w_valid,
    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,
    input               a_valid,
    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,
    output              c_valid,
    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data
    );

   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;
   localparam ACW = 19;         // internal accumulator width

   wire ld = w_valid;
   wire en = ~w_valid;

      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew

              if (en)

         end
      end
   endgenerate

     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, then sign-extend to ACCW
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACW +: ACW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-ACW){cin[ACW-1]}}, cin};
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [D*ACW-1:0] dr;
            always @(posedge clk)
              if (en)
                dr <= {dr[(D-1)*ACW-1:0], cin};
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =
                   {{(ACCW-ACW){dr[D*ACW-1]}}, dr[D*ACW-1 -: ACW]};
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end

[ASSISTANT]
FOUND IT! Classic Verilog width trap: `c1_q[0] & a19` **zero-extends** the 1-bit select to 19 bits (`{18'b0, c1_q[0]}`), so the AND only ever passes bit 0 of `a19` — exactly the "LSB-only" symptom (values 1, 2 = kv + ~a[0]). I need explicit **replication** `{ACCW{c1_q[0]}}` like in my working designs:
  -> edit_file(filename='design7.v', old_string='   wire [ACCW-1:0] y0 = (c1_q[0] & a19) | (c2_q[0] & a219)\n                      | (cn1_q[0] & a19n) | (cn2_q[0] & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y1 = (c1_q[1] & a19) | (c2_q[1] & a219)\n                      | (cn1_q[1] & a19n) | (cn2_q[1] & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y2 = (c1_q[2] & a19) | (c2_q[2] & a219)\n                      | (cn1_q[2] & a19n) | (cn2_q[2] & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y3 = (c1_q[3] & a19) | (c2_q[3] & a219)\n                      | (cn1_q[3] & a19n) | (cn2_q[3] & a219n);', new_string='   wire [ACCW-1:0] y0 = ({ACCW{c1_q[0]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[0]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[0]}} & a19n) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[0]}} & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y1 = ({ACCW{c1_q[1]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[1]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[1]}} & a19n) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[1]}} & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y2 = ({ACCW{c1_q[2]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[2]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[2]}} & a19n) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[2]}} & a219n);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y3 = ({ACCW{c1_q[3]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[3]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[3]}} & a19n) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[3]}} & a219n);')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design7.v')

[ASSISTANT]
PASS — design7 fixed gives ADP **2.54M** (delay 550, area 4621) — essentially tied with design6 (2.533M).

Now a bigger structural idea: kill the `kv` correction vector entirely by computing **NA = −A once per PE** (`~A+1`, one small incrementer) and using the one-hot AND-OR over `{A, 2A, −A, −2A}`. Then rows are *exact* (no +1 corrections), leaving just **6 vectors → a 3-level tree** (was 4 levels):
  -> create_file(content="// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to\n// logikbench `tpu`.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:\n//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 19 bits (exact: |product| <= 2^14 and\n//    every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so |sum| <= 2^17; the golden\n//    32-bit datapath never wraps, so sign extension reproduces it exactly).\n//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is); all\n//    datapath state is overwritten with defined values before the first\n//    c_valid pulse, and c_data is only checked while c_valid is high.\n//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a\n//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2\n//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column\n//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.\n//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The Booth digit of\n//    each weight pair is decoded at LOAD time into a one-hot row control\n//    {+1,+2,-1,-2} (registered).  The four row sources A, 2A, -A, -2A are\n//    formed once per cycle (-A = ~A+1 with a single incrementer shared by\n//    all four digits, -2A = -A << 1), so rows are EXACT and no +1\n//    correction vector is needed:\n//      y_i = (c1 & A) | (c2 & 2A) | (cn1 & -A) | (cn2 & -2A),  row_i = y_i<<2i\n//    Only 6 vectors (4 rows + ps + pc) feed a 3-level 3:2 compressor.\n//    All arithmetic is exact modulo 2^19, matching the golden.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // accumulator width\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input               en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   ps_n,       // partial-sum vector in from above\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   pc_n,       // partial-carry vector in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] ps_s,     // partial-sum vector out to below\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] pc_s      // partial-carry vector out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- Booth radix-4 digit decode of the weight (registered at load) --\n   // digit i: d_i = w[2i-1] + w[2i] - 2*w[2i+1]   (w[-1] = 0)\n   wire [3:0] bh = {w_north[7], w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1]};\n   wire [3:0] bm = {w_north[6], w_north[4], w_north[2], w_north[0]};\n   wire [3:0] bl = {w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [3:0] bc1  = ~bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = +1\n   wire [3:0] bc2  = ~bh & bm & bl;     // d = +2\n   wire [3:0] bcn1 =  bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = -1\n   wire [3:0] bcn2 =  bh & ~bm & ~bl;   // d = -2\n\n   reg [3:0] c1_q, c2_q, cn1_q, cn2_q;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w     <= w_north;\n         c1_q  <= bc1;\n         c2_q  <= bc2;\n         cn1_q <= bcn1;\n         cn2_q <= bcn2;\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         ps_s   <= L3a[ACCW-1:0];\n         pc_s   <= L3a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW];\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- row sources: A, 2A, -A, -2A  (exact, no +1 corrections) -------\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a19   = {{(ACCW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a219  = a19 << 1;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na19  = ~a19 + 1'b1;      // -A, shared by all digits\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na219 = na19 << 1;        // -2A\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y0 = ({ACCW{c1_q[0]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[0]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[0]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[0]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y1 = ({ACCW{c1_q[1]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[1]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[1]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[1]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y2 = ({ACCW{c1_q[2]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[2]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[2]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[2]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y3 = ({ACCW{c1_q[3]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[3]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[3]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[3]}} & na219);\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r0 = y0;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r1 = y1 << 2;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r2 = y2 << 4;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r3 = y3 << 6;\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressors ----------------------------------------------\n   function [2*ACCW-1:0] fa3;\n      input [ACCW-1:0] a, b, c;\n      reg   [ACCW-1:0] s, ch;\n      begin\n         s    = a ^ b ^ c;\n         ch   = (a & b) | (a & c) | (b & c);\n         fa3  = {{ch[ACCW-2:0], 1'b0}, s};   // carry-out of MSB dropped\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 vectors\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1a = fa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1b = fa3(r3, ps_n, pc_n);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L2a = fa3(L1a[ACCW-1:0], L1a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[ACCW-1:0]);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L3a = fa3(L2a[ACCW-1:0], L2a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[2*ACCW-1:ACCW]);\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]     a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]     w_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar            i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west  (a_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_n    (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_n    (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east  (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_s    (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_s    (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACW = 19;         // internal accumulator width\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zero fill when a_valid low\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar          i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [i*DW-1:0] sr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                sr <= {sr[(i-1)*DW-1:0], din};\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i*DW-1 -: DW];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // the systolic array\n   wire [N*ACW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, then sign-extend to ACCW\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACW +: ACW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-ACW){cin[ACW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [D*ACW-1:0] dr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                dr <= {dr[(D-1)*ACW-1:0], cin};\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-ACW){dr[D*ACW-1]}}, dr[D*ACW-1 -: ACW]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design8.v')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design8.v')

[ASSISTANT]
target_delay=700 is worse (585ps) — the default 500 mapping is better. The row-gen's incrementer+AND-OR (~320ps) is the bottleneck.

**Key idea:** the negation `−A` only needs ONE incrementer per *row input*, not per PE! Pass both `A` and `−A` eastward through the array (a 9-bit NA path registered alongside a_east). Then every PE's row generator is a pure register-to-register AND-OR over `{A, 2A, −A, −2A}` — no incrementer in the PEs at all:
  -> create_file(content="// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to\n// logikbench `tpu`.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:\n//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 19 bits (exact: |product| <= 2^14 and\n//    every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so |sum| <= 2^17; the golden\n//    32-bit datapath never wraps, so sign extension reproduces it exactly).\n//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is); all\n//    datapath state is overwritten with defined values before the first\n//    c_valid pulse, and c_data is only checked while c_valid is high.\n//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a\n//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2\n//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column\n//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.\n//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The Booth digit of\n//    each weight pair is decoded at LOAD time into a one-hot row control\n//    {+1,+2,-1,-2} (registered).  The negated activation -A is computed ONCE\n//    per array row at the west edge (8 incrementers total) and flows east\n//    next to A, so each PE's row generator is a pure AND-OR of register\n//    outputs over {A, 2A, -A, -2A} (2A and -2A are shifts, free):\n//      y_i = (c1 & A) | (c2 & 2A) | (cn1 & -A) | (cn2 & -2A),  row_i = y_i<<2i\n//    Rows are exact, so no +1 correction vector is needed: only 6 vectors\n//    (4 rows + ps + pc) feed a 3-level 3:2 compressor.  All arithmetic is\n//    exact modulo 2^19, matching the golden.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // accumulator width\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input               en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [DW:0]       na_west,    // -activation in from west (9 bit exact)\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   ps_n,       // partial-sum vector in from above\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   pc_n,       // partial-carry vector in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [DW:0]   na_east,    // -activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] ps_s,     // partial-sum vector out to below\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] pc_s      // partial-carry vector out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- Booth radix-4 digit decode of the weight (registered at load) --\n   // digit i: d_i = w[2i-1] + w[2i] - 2*w[2i+1]   (w[-1] = 0)\n   wire [3:0] bh = {w_north[7], w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1]};\n   wire [3:0] bm = {w_north[6], w_north[4], w_north[2], w_north[0]};\n   wire [3:0] bl = {w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [3:0] bc1  = ~bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = +1\n   wire [3:0] bc2  = ~bh & bm & bl;     // d = +2\n   wire [3:0] bcn1 =  bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = -1\n   wire [3:0] bcn2 =  bh & ~bm & ~bl;   // d = -2\n\n   reg [3:0] c1_q, c2_q, cn1_q, cn2_q;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w     <= w_north;\n         c1_q  <= bc1;\n         c2_q  <= bc2;\n         cn1_q <= bcn1;\n         cn2_q <= bcn2;\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         na_east <= na_west;\n         ps_s    <= L3a[ACCW-1:0];\n         pc_s    <= L3a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW];\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- row sources: A, 2A, -A, -2A (all straight from registers) -----\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a19   = {{(ACCW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a219  = a19 << 1;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na19  = {{(ACCW-DW-1){na_west[DW]}}, na_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na219 = na19 << 1;\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y0 = ({ACCW{c1_q[0]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[0]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[0]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[0]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y1 = ({ACCW{c1_q[1]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[1]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[1]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[1]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y2 = ({ACCW{c1_q[2]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[2]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[2]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[2]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y3 = ({ACCW{c1_q[3]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[3]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[3]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[3]}} & na219);\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r0 = y0;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r1 = y1 << 2;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r2 = y2 << 4;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r3 = y3 << 6;\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressors ----------------------------------------------\n   function [2*ACCW-1:0] fa3;\n      input [ACCW-1:0] a, b, c;\n      reg   [ACCW-1:0] s, ch;\n      begin\n         s    = a ^ b ^ c;\n         ch   = (a & b) | (a & c) | (b & c);\n         fa3  = {{ch[ACCW-2:0], 1'b0}, s};   // carry-out of MSB dropped\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 vectors\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1a = fa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1b = fa3(r3, ps_n, pc_n);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L2a = fa3(L1a[ACCW-1:0], L1a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[ACCW-1:0]);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L3a = fa3(L2a[ACCW-1:0], L2a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[2*ACCW-1:ACCW]);\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]     a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW:0]       na_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]     w_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar            i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         wire [DW:0] a9 = {a_left[(i+1)*DW-1], a_left[i*DW +: DW]};\n         assign a_h[i][0]  = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n         assign na_h[i][0] = ~a9 + 1'b1;      // -A, computed once per row\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west  (a_h[i][j]),\n               .na_west (na_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_n    (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_n    (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east  (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .na_east (na_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_s    (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_s    (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACW = 19;         // internal accumulator width\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zero fill when a_valid low\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar          i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [i*DW-1:0] sr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                sr <= {sr[(i-1)*DW-1:0], din};\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i*DW-1 -: DW];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // the systolic array\n   wire [N*ACW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, then sign-extend to ACCW\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACW +: ACW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-ACW){cin[ACW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [D*ACW-1:0] dr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                dr <= {dr[(D-1)*ACW-1:0], cin};\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-ACW){dr[D*ACW-1]}}, dr[D*ACW-1 -: ACW]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design9.v')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design9.v')

[ASSISTANT]
PASS but ADP 2.468M — marginally worse than design8. The incrementer is still on the critical path because PE(i,0) computes `−A` from the skew output combinationally. 

Fix: compute `−A` **at the skew input** (before the skew registers) and shift the (A, −A) pair through the skew chain too — then *every* PE row-gen input is a pure register output:
  -> create_file(content="// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to\n// logikbench `tpu`.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:\n//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 19 bits (exact: |product| <= 2^14 and\n//    every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so |sum| <= 2^17; the golden\n//    32-bit datapath never wraps, so sign extension reproduces it exactly).\n//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is); all\n//    datapath state is overwritten with defined values before the first\n//    c_valid pulse, and c_data is only checked while c_valid is high.\n//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a\n//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2\n//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column\n//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.\n//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The Booth digit of\n//    each weight pair is decoded at LOAD time into a one-hot row control\n//    {+1,+2,-1,-2} (registered).  The negated activation -A is computed once\n//    at the activation input and REGISTERED through the input skew and the\n//    east-west chain alongside A, so every PE row generator is a pure\n//    AND-OR of register outputs over {A, 2A, -A, -2A} (2A and -2A are\n//    shifts, free):\n//      y_i = (c1 & A) | (c2 & 2A) | (cn1 & -A) | (cn2 & -2A),  row_i = y_i<<2i\n//    Rows are exact, so no +1 correction vector is needed: only 6 vectors\n//    (4 rows + ps + pc) feed a 3-level 3:2 compressor.  All arithmetic is\n//    exact modulo 2^19, matching the golden.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // accumulator width\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input               en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]     w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [DW:0]       na_west,    // -activation in from west (9 bit exact)\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   ps_n,       // partial-sum vector in from above\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   pc_n,       // partial-carry vector in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]     w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [DW:0]   na_east,    // -activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] ps_s,     // partial-sum vector out to below\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] pc_s      // partial-carry vector out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- Booth radix-4 digit decode of the weight (registered at load) --\n   // digit i: d_i = w[2i-1] + w[2i] - 2*w[2i+1]   (w[-1] = 0)\n   wire [3:0] bh = {w_north[7], w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1]};\n   wire [3:0] bm = {w_north[6], w_north[4], w_north[2], w_north[0]};\n   wire [3:0] bl = {w_north[5], w_north[3], w_north[1], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [3:0] bc1  = ~bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = +1\n   wire [3:0] bc2  = ~bh & bm & bl;     // d = +2\n   wire [3:0] bcn1 =  bh & (bm ^ bl);   // d = -1\n   wire [3:0] bcn2 =  bh & ~bm & ~bl;   // d = -2\n\n   reg [3:0] c1_q, c2_q, cn1_q, cn2_q;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         w     <= w_north;\n         c1_q  <= bc1;\n         c2_q  <= bc2;\n         cn1_q <= bcn1;\n         cn2_q <= bcn2;\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         na_east <= na_west;\n         ps_s    <= L3a[ACCW-1:0];\n         pc_s    <= L3a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW];\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- row sources: A, 2A, -A, -2A (all straight from registers) -----\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a19   = {{(ACCW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a219  = a19 << 1;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na19  = {{(ACCW-DW-1){na_west[DW]}}, na_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na219 = na19 << 1;\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y0 = ({ACCW{c1_q[0]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[0]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[0]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[0]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y1 = ({ACCW{c1_q[1]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[1]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[1]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[1]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y2 = ({ACCW{c1_q[2]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[2]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[2]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[2]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y3 = ({ACCW{c1_q[3]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2_q[3]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1_q[3]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2_q[3]}} & na219);\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r0 = y0;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r1 = y1 << 2;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r2 = y2 << 4;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r3 = y3 << 6;\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressors ----------------------------------------------\n   function [2*ACCW-1:0] fa3;\n      input [ACCW-1:0] a, b, c;\n      reg   [ACCW-1:0] s, ch;\n      begin\n         s    = a ^ b ^ c;\n         ch   = (a & b) | (a & c) | (b & c);\n         fa3  = {{ch[ACCW-2:0], 1'b0}, s};   // carry-out of MSB dropped\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 vectors\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1a = fa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1b = fa3(r3, ps_n, pc_n);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L2a = fa3(L1a[ACCW-1:0], L1a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[ACCW-1:0]);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L3a = fa3(L2a[ACCW-1:0], L2a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[2*ACCW-1:ACCW]);\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    input  [N*(DW+1)-1:0] na_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]     a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW:0]       na_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]     w_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar            i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0]  = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n         assign na_h[i][0] = na_left[i*(DW+1) +: (DW+1)];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk     (clk),\n               .ld      (ld),\n               .en      (en),\n               .w_north (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west  (a_h[i][j]),\n               .na_west (na_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_n    (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_n    (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east  (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .na_east (na_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_s    (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_s    (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACW = 19;         // internal accumulator width\n   localparam NAW = DW + 1;     // exact -A width\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zero fill when a_valid low.\n   // The negated activation -A is computed at the input and skewed in\n   // parallel so that every PE sees registered (A, -A) pairs.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0]   a_left;\n   wire [N*NAW-1:0]  na_left;\n   genvar            i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0]  din  = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         wire [NAW-1:0] dinn = ~{din[DW-1], din} + 1'b1;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = din;\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = dinn;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [i*DW-1:0]  sr;\n            reg [i*NAW-1:0] srn;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en) begin\n                 sr  <= {sr[(i-1)*DW-1:0], din};\n                 srn <= {srn[(i-1)*NAW-1:0], dinn};\n              end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = sr[i*DW-1 -: DW];\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = srn[i*NAW-1 -: NAW];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // the systolic array\n   wire [N*ACW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .na_left(na_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, then sign-extend to ACCW\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACW +: ACW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-ACW){cin[ACW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [D*ACW-1:0] dr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                dr <= {dr[(D-1)*ACW-1:0], cin};\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-ACW){dr[D*ACW-1]}}, dr[D*ACW-1 -: ACW]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design10.v')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design10.v')

[ASSISTANT]
target 560 gives the identical mapping — the knob is inert here. 

Now an area idea: in design10 the PE stores **both** the raw weight `w` (8 bits, only used to feed the load shift chain) **and** the Booth controls (16 bits) = 24 FF/PE. Instead, decode the weight into a compact 3-bit Booth code per digit at the array's top edge and shift *that* south — 12 FF/PE, no `w` register at all. The code `{one, two, neg}` gives the row controls with single gates:
  -> create_file(content="// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to\n// logikbench `tpu`.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:\n//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 19 bits (exact: |product| <= 2^14 and\n//    every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so |sum| <= 2^17; the golden\n//    32-bit datapath never wraps, so sign extension reproduces it exactly).\n//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is); all\n//    datapath state is overwritten with defined values before the first\n//    c_valid pulse, and c_data is only checked while c_valid is high.\n//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a\n//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2\n//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column\n//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.\n//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The weight is\n//    decoded ONCE at the array top edge into 4 Booth digits, each stored as\n//    3 bits {one, two, neg}:  d = +-1 (one), +-2 (two), sign (neg), zero\n//    (neither).  The 12-bit code shifts south during load (no raw weight\n//    register needed in the PEs).  The negated activation -A is computed\n//    once at the activation input and registered through the input skew and\n//    the east-west chain alongside A, so every PE row generator is a pure\n//    AND-OR of register outputs over {A, 2A, -A, -2A} (2A, -2A are shifts):\n//      y_i = (one&~neg & A) | (two&~neg & 2A) | (one&neg & -A) | (two&neg & -2A)\n//    Rows are exact (no +1 corrections): 6 vectors (4 rows + ps + pc) feed\n//    a 3-level 3:2 compressor.  All arithmetic is exact modulo 2^19,\n//    matching the golden.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // accumulator width\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input               en,         // compute enable\n    input  [11:0]       ctl_north,  // Booth code in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [DW:0]       na_west,    // -activation in from west (9 bit exact)\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   ps_n,       // partial-sum vector in from above\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   pc_n,       // partial-carry vector in from above\n    output [11:0]       ctl_south,  // Booth code out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [DW:0]   na_east,    // -activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] ps_s,     // partial-sum vector out to below\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] pc_s      // partial-carry vector out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [11:0] ctl;\n   assign ctl_south = ctl;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        ctl <= ctl_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         na_east <= na_west;\n         ps_s    <= L3a[ACCW-1:0];\n         pc_s    <= L3a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW];\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- row sources: A, 2A, -A, -2A (all straight from registers) -----\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a19   = {{(ACCW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a219  = a19 << 1;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na19  = {{(ACCW-DW-1){na_west[DW]}}, na_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na219 = na19 << 1;\n\n   // ---- row generation: AND-OR of one-hot row selects ------------------\n   wire [3:0] one = {ctl[9], ctl[6], ctl[3], ctl[0]};   // |d| == 1\n   wire [3:0] two = {ctl[10], ctl[7], ctl[4], ctl[1]};  // |d| == 2\n   wire [3:0] neg = {ctl[11], ctl[8], ctl[5], ctl[2]};  // d < 0\n\n   wire [3:0] c1  =  one & ~neg;   // d = +1\n   wire [3:0] c2  =  two & ~neg;   // d = +2\n   wire [3:0] cn1 =  one &  neg;   // d = -1\n   wire [3:0] cn2 =  two &  neg;   // d = -2\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y0 = ({ACCW{c1[0]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2[0]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1[0]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2[0]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y1 = ({ACCW{c1[1]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2[1]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1[1]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2[1]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y2 = ({ACCW{c1[2]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2[2]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1[2]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2[2]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y3 = ({ACCW{c1[3]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{c2[3]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{cn1[3]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{cn2[3]}} & na219);\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r0 = y0;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r1 = y1 << 2;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r2 = y2 << 4;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r3 = y3 << 6;\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressors ----------------------------------------------\n   function [2*ACCW-1:0] fa3;\n      input [ACCW-1:0] a, b, c;\n      reg   [ACCW-1:0] s, ch;\n      begin\n         s    = a ^ b ^ c;\n         ch   = (a & b) | (a & c) | (b & c);\n         fa3  = {{ch[ACCW-2:0], 1'b0}, s};   // carry-out of MSB dropped\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 vectors\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1a = fa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1b = fa3(r3, ps_n, pc_n);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L2a = fa3(L1a[ACCW-1:0], L1a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[ACCW-1:0]);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L3a = fa3(L2a[ACCW-1:0], L2a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[2*ACCW-1:ACCW]);\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    input  [N*(DW+1)-1:0] na_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [11:0]       ctl_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]     a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW:0]       na_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar            i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0]  = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n         assign na_h[i][0] = na_left[i*(DW+1) +: (DW+1)];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         // Booth digit decode of the weight row (load time, off critical path)\n         wire [DW-1:0] w  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         wire [3:0]    bh = {w[7], w[5], w[3], w[1]};\n         wire [3:0]    bm = {w[6], w[4], w[2], w[0]};\n         wire [3:0]    bl = {w[5], w[3], w[1], 1'b0};\n         wire [3:0]    bone = bm ^ bl;                       // |d| == 1\n         wire [3:0]    btwo = (bh & ~bm & ~bl) | (~bh & bm & bl);  // |d| == 2\n         wire [3:0]    bneg = bh & ~(bm & bl);               // d < 0\n         assign ctl_v[0][j] = {bneg[3], btwo[3], bone[3], bneg[2], btwo[2],\n                               bone[2], bneg[1], btwo[1], bone[1], bneg[0],\n                               btwo[0], bone[0]};\n\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk       (clk),\n               .ld        (ld),\n               .en        (en),\n               .ctl_north (ctl_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west    (a_h[i][j]),\n               .na_west   (na_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_n      (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_n      (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .ctl_south (ctl_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east    (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .na_east   (na_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_s      (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_s      (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACW = 19;         // internal accumulator width\n   localparam NAW = DW + 1;     // exact -A width\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zero fill when a_valid low.\n   // The negated activation -A is computed at the input and skewed in\n   // parallel so that every PE sees registered (A, -A) pairs.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0]   a_left;\n   wire [N*NAW-1:0]  na_left;\n   genvar            i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0]  din  = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         wire [NAW-1:0] dinn = ~{din[DW-1], din} + 1'b1;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = din;\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = dinn;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [i*DW-1:0]  sr;\n            reg [i*NAW-1:0] srn;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en) begin\n                 sr  <= {sr[(i-1)*DW-1:0], din};\n                 srn <= {srn[(i-1)*NAW-1:0], dinn};\n              end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = sr[i*DW-1 -: DW];\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = srn[i*NAW-1 -: NAW];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // the systolic array\n   wire [N*ACW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .na_left(na_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, then sign-extend to ACCW\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACW +: ACW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-ACW){cin[ACW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [D*ACW-1:0] dr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                dr <= {dr[(D-1)*ACW-1:0], cin};\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-ACW){dr[D*ACW-1]}}, dr[D*ACW-1 -: ACW]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design11.v')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design11.v')

PASS — design11: area 4533 (−88) but delay 526 (+16) — the code decode adds a level. Net 2.384M, slightly worse than design10.

The safe variant: shift a **16-bit one-hot** code (no `w` register, no decode on the compute path) — keeps design10's 510ps delay while still removing the 8-bit weight register per PE:
  -> create_file(content="// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to\n// logikbench `tpu`.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:\n//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 19 bits (exact: |product| <= 2^14 and\n//    every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so |sum| <= 2^17; the golden\n//    32-bit datapath never wraps, so sign extension reproduces it exactly).\n//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is); all\n//    datapath state is overwritten with defined values before the first\n//    c_valid pulse, and c_data is only checked while c_valid is high.\n//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a\n//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2\n//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column\n//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.\n//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The weight is\n//    decoded ONCE at the array top edge into a one-hot row control per digit\n//    {+1,+2,-1,-2} (zero digit = all zero) and that 16-bit code is what\n//    shifts south during load (no raw weight register in the PEs).  The\n//    negated activation -A is computed once at the activation input and\n//    registered through the input skew and the east-west chain alongside A,\n//    so every PE row generator is a pure AND-OR of register outputs over\n//    {A, 2A, -A, -2A} (2A, -2A are shifts):\n//      y_i = (c1 & A) | (c2 & 2A) | (cn1 & -A) | (cn2 & -2A), row_i = y_i<<2i\n//    Rows are exact (no +1 corrections): 6 vectors (4 rows + ps + pc) feed\n//    a 3-level 3:2 compressor.     | ({ACCW{ctl[6]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{ctl[7]}} & na219);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] y2 = ({ACCW{ctl[8]}} & a19) | ({ACCW{ctl[9]}} & a219)\n                      | ({ACCW{ctl[10]}} & na19) | ({ACCW{ctl[11]}}             bcn2[0], bcn1[0], bc2[0], bc1[0]};\n\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk       (clk),\n               .ld        (ld),\n               .en        (en),\n               .ctl_north (ctl_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west    (a_h[i][j]),\n               .na_west   (na_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_n      (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_n      (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .ctl_south (ctl_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east    (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .na_east   (na_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_s      (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_s      (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACW = 19;         // internal accumulator width\n   localparam NAW = DW + 1;     // exact -A width\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zero fill when a_valid low.\n   // The negated activation -A is computed at the input and skewed in\n   // parallel so that every PE sees registered (A, -A) pairs.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0]   a_left;\n   wire [N*NAW-1:0]  na_left;\n   genvar            i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0]  din  = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         wire [NAW-1:0] dinn = ~{din[DW-1], din} + 1'b1;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = din;\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = dinn;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [i*DW-1:0]  sr;\n            reg [i*NAW-1:0] srn;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en) begin\n                 sr  <= {sr[(i-1)*DW-1:0], din};\n                 srn <= {srn[(i-1)*NAW-1:0], dinn};\n              end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = sr[i*DW-1 -: DW];\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = srn[i*NAW-1 -: NAW];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // the systolic array\n   wire [N*ACW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .na_left(na_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, then sign-extend to ACCW\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACW +: ACW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-ACW){cin[ACW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [D*ACW-1:0] dr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                dr <= {dr[(D-1)*ACW-1:0], cin};\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-ACW){dr[D*ACW-1]}}, dr[D*ACW-1 -: ACW]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design12.v')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design12.v')

[ASSISTANT]
target 440 is also inert — 495ps is the structural floor. Design12 stays best at **2.177M**.

Next: a better factored row generator. Since the one-hot digit makes `U=|d|=1 row` and `V=|d|=2 row` **mutually exclusive**, the row is `r_i = (U << 2i) | (V << 2i+1)` where both U and V share ONE mux `X = neg ? −A : A`:
`U = one & X`, `V = two & X`. That's fewer, shallower gates and lets the ctl code shrink to 12 FF/PE:
  -> create_file(content="// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to\n// logikbench `tpu`.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:\n//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 19 bits (exact: |product| <= 2^14 and\n//    every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so |sum| <= 2^17; the golden\n//    32-bit datapath never wraps, so sign extension reproduces it exactly).\n//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is); all\n//    datapath state is overwritten with defined values before the first\n//    c_valid pulse, and c_data is only checked while c_valid is high.\n//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a\n//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2\n//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column\n//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.\n//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The weight is\n//    decoded ONCE at the array top edge into 4 Booth digits, each stored as\n//    3 bits {one, two, neg} (12-bit code shifts south during load; no raw\n//    weight register in the PEs).  The negated activation -A is computed\n//    once at the activation input and registered through the input skew and\n//    the east-west chain alongside A.  Each digit's row is then\n//      X = neg ? -A : A,   U = one & X,   V = two & X,   row = (U | V<<1) << 2i\n//    (U and V are mutually exclusive, so the OR is exact).  Rows are exact,\n//    no +1 corrections: 6 vectors (4 rows + ps + pc) feed a 3-level 3:2\n//    compressor.  All arithmetic is exact modulo 2^19, matching the golden.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // accumulator width\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input               en,         // compute enable\n    input  [11:0]       ctl_north,  // Booth code in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [DW:0]       na_west,    // -activation in from west (9 bit exact)\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   ps_n,       // partial-sum vector in from above\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   pc_n,       // partial-carry vector in from above\n    output [11:0]       ctl_south,  // Booth code out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [DW:0]   na_east,    // -activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] ps_s,     // partial-sum vector out to below\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] pc_s      // partial-carry vector out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [11:0] ctl;\n   assign ctl_south = ctl;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        ctl <= ctl_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         na_east <= na_west;\n         ps_s    <= L3a[ACCW-1:0];\n         pc_s    <= L3a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW];\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- row sources: A, -A (2A and -2A are shifts) --------------------\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a19  = {{(ACCW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na19 = {{(ACCW-DW-1){na_west[DW]}}, na_west};\n\n   wire [3:0] one = {ctl[9], ctl[6], ctl[3], ctl[0]};   // |d| == 1\n   wire [3:0] two = {ctl[10], ctl[7], ctl[4], ctl[1]};  // |d| == 2\n   wire [3:0] neg = {ctl[11], ctl[8], ctl[5], ctl[2]};  // d < 0\n\n   // ---- row generation: X = neg ? -A : A;  row = (one&X | two&X<<1)<<2i\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] x0 = neg[0] ? na19 : a19;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] x1 = neg[1] ? na19 : a19;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] x2 = neg[2] ? na19 : a19;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] x3 = neg[3] ? na19 : a19;\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r0 = ({ACCW{one[0]}} & x0) | (({ACCW{two[0]}} & x0) << 1);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r1 = (({ACCW{one[1]}} & x1) | (({ACCW{two[1]}} & x1) << 1)) << 2;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r2 = (({ACCW{one[2]}} & x2) | (({ACCW{two[2]}} & x2) << 1)) << 4;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r3 = (({ACCW{one[3]}} & x3) | (({ACCW{two[3]}} & x3) << 1)) << 6;\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressors ----------------------------------------------\n   function [2*ACCW-1:0] fa3;\n      input [ACCW-1:0] a, b, c;\n      reg   [ACCW-1:0] s, ch;\n      begin\n         s    = a ^ b ^ c;\n         ch   = (a & b) | (a & c) | (b & c);\n         fa3  = {{ch[ACCW-2:0], 1'b0}, s};   // carry-out of MSB dropped\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 vectors\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1a = fa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1b = fa3(r3, ps_n, pc_n);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L2a = fa3(L1a[ACCW-1:0], L1a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[ACCW-1:0]);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L3a = fa3(L2a[ACCW-1:0], L2a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[2*ACCW-1:ACCW]);\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    input  [N*(DW+1)-1:0] na_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [11:0]       ctl_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]     a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW:0]       na_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar            i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0]  = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n         assign na_h[i][0] = na_left[i*(DW+1) +: (DW+1)];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         // Booth digit decode of the weight row (load time, off critical path)\n         // digit i: d_i = w[2i-1] + w[2i] - 2*w[2i+1]   (w[-1] = 0)\n         wire [DW-1:0] w  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         wire [3:0]    bh = {w[7], w[5], w[3], w[1]};\n         wire [3:0]    bm = {w[6], w[4], w[2], w[0]};\n         wire [3:0]    bl = {w[5], w[3], w[1], 1'b0};\n         wire [3:0]    bone = bm ^ bl;                             // |d| == 1\n         wire [3:0]    btwo = (bh & ~bm & ~bl) | (~bh & bm & bl);  // |d| == 2\n         wire [3:0]    bneg = bh & ~(bm & bl);                     // d < 0\n         assign ctl_v[0][j] = {bneg[3], btwo[3], bone[3], bneg[2], btwo[2],\n                               bone[2], bneg[1], btwo[1], bone[1], bneg[0],\n                               btwo[0], bone[0]};\n\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk       (clk),\n               .ld        (ld),\n               .en        (en),\n               .ctl_north (ctl_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west    (a_h[i][j]),\n               .na_west   (na_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_n      (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_n      (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .ctl_south (ctl_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east    (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .na_east   (na_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_s      (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_s      (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACW = 19;         // internal accumulator width\n   localparam NAW = DW + 1;     // exact -A width\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zero fill when a_valid low.\n   // The negated activation -A is computed at the input and skewed in\n   // parallel so that every PE sees registered (A, -A) pairs.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0]   a_left;\n   wire [N*NAW-1:0]  na_left;\n   genvar            i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0]  din  = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         wire [NAW-1:0] dinn = ~{din[DW-1], din} + 1'b1;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = din;\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = dinn;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [i*DW-1:0]  sr;\n            reg [i*NAW-1:0] srn;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en) begin\n                 sr  <= {sr[(i-1)*DW-1:0], din};\n                 srn <= {srn[(i-1)*NAW-1:0], dinn};\n              end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = sr[i*DW-1 -: DW];\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = srn[i*NAW-1 -: NAW];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // the systolic array\n   wire [N*ACW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .na_left(na_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, then sign-extend to ACCW\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACW +: ACW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-ACW){cin[ACW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [D*ACW-1:0] dr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                dr <= {dr[(D-1)*ACW-1:0], cin};\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-ACW){dr[D*ACW-1]}}, dr[D*ACW-1 -: ACW]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design13.v')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design13.v')
