[ASSISTANT]
Confirmed at **2.1735M**. Now a new idea to shave area: the accumulator only needs 18 bits! The reachable result range is [−130048, +131072] (min product is −16256, not −16384), which injectively fits mod 2^18; the single colliding value (+131072) is decoded by one extra bit at the bottom. That shrinks the tree, row-gen and accumulator pair by 1 bit each (~5%):
  -> create_file(content="// Weight-stationary systolic matmul tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate to\n// logikbench `tpu`.\n//\n// Optimizations vs. the original RTL:\n//  * internal accumulator width 32 -> 18 bits.  Each product is <= 2^14 in\n//    magnitude and every partial sum holds <= 8 products, so V is in\n//    [-130048, +131072]; all those values are distinct modulo 2^18 (the\n//    range is narrower than 2^18), so a redundant pair of 18-bit vectors\n//    (sum, carry) represents every partial sum exactly, and the golden's\n//    32-bit datapath never wraps.  The one value at +131072 is recovered at\n//    the bottom by the extra bit sel = sum[17] & |sum[16:10].\n//  * datapath registers are not reset (only the valid pipeline is).\n//  * carry-save accumulation: the partial sum travels down each column as a\n//    redundant (sum, carry) pair merged with the local product by 3:2\n//    compressors (no carry chains).  One carry-propagate adder per column\n//    converts the pair at the bottom of the array.\n//  * radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate.  The weight is\n//    decoded ONCE at the array top edge into 4 Booth digits, each stored as\n//    3 bits {one, two, neg} (12-bit code shifts south during load; no raw\n//    weight register in the PEs).  The negated activation -A is computed\n//    once at the activation input and registered through the input skew and\n//    the east-west chain alongside A.  Each digit's row is\n//      X = neg ? -A : A,   row = (one&X | two&X<<1) << 2i\n//    (one and two are mutually exclusive, so the OR is exact).  Rows are\n//    exact, no +1 corrections: 6 vectors (4 rows + ps + pc) feed a 3-level\n//    3:2 compressor.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 18) // accumulator width\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input               en,         // compute enable\n    input  [11:0]       ctl_north,  // Booth code in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]     a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [DW:0]       na_west,    // -activation in from west (9 bit exact)\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   ps_n,       // partial-sum vector in from above\n    input  [ACCW-1:0]   pc_n,       // partial-carry vector in from above\n    output [11:0]       ctl_south,  // Booth code out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0] a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [DW:0]   na_east,    // -activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] ps_s,     // partial-sum vector out to below\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] pc_s      // partial-carry vector out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [11:0] ctl;\n   assign ctl_south = ctl;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        ctl <= ctl_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         na_east <= na_west;\n         ps_s    <= L3a[ACCW-1:0];\n         pc_s    <= L3a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW];\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- row sources: A, -A (2A and -2A are shifts) --------------------\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] a19  = {{(ACCW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] na19 = {{(ACCW-DW-1){na_west[DW]}}, na_west};\n\n   wire [3:0] one = {ctl[9], ctl[6], ctl[3], ctl[0]};   // |d| == 1\n   wire [3:0] two = {ctl[10], ctl[7], ctl[4], ctl[1]};  // |d| == 2\n   wire [3:0] neg = {ctl[11], ctl[8], ctl[5], ctl[2]};  // d < 0\n\n   // ---- row generation: X = neg ? -A : A;  row = (one&X | two&X<<1)<<2i\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] x0 = neg[0] ? na19 : a19;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] x1 = neg[1] ? na19 : a19;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] x2 = neg[2] ? na19 : a19;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] x3 = neg[3] ? na19 : a19;\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r0 = ({ACCW{one[0]}} & x0) | (({ACCW{two[0]}} & x0) << 1);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r1 = (({ACCW{one[1]}} & x1) | (({ACCW{two[1]}} & x1) << 1)) << 2;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r2 = (({ACCW{one[2]}} & x2) | (({ACCW{two[2]}} & x2) << 1)) << 4;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] r3 = (({ACCW{one[3]}} & x3) | (({ACCW{two[3]}} & x3) << 1)) << 6;\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressors ----------------------------------------------\n   function [2*ACCW-1:0] fa3;\n      input [ACCW-1:0] a, b, c;\n      reg   [ACCW-1:0] s, ch;\n      begin\n         s    = a ^ b ^ c;\n         ch   = (a & b) | (a & c) | (b & c);\n         fa3  = {{ch[ACCW-2:0], 1'b0}, s};\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // 6 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2 vectors\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1a = fa3(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L1b = fa3(r3, ps_n, pc_n);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L2a = fa3(L1a[ACCW-1:0], L1a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[ACCW-1:0]);\n\n   wire [2*ACCW-1:0] L3a = fa3(L2a[ACCW-1:0], L2a[2*ACCW-1:ACCW], L1b[2*ACCW-1:ACCW]);\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 18)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               ld,\n    input               en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_left,\n    input  [N*(DW+1)-1:0] na_left,\n    output [N*(ACCW+1)-1:0] c_bot   // 19-bit results\n    );\n\n   wire [11:0]       ctl_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]     a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW:0]       na_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   ps_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0]   pc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar            i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0]  = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n         assign na_h[i][0] = na_left[i*(DW+1) +: (DW+1)];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         // Booth digit decode of the weight row (load time, off critical path)\n         // digit i: d_i = w[2i-1] + w[2i] - 2*w[2i+1]   (w[-1] = 0)\n         wire [DW-1:0] w  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         wire [3:0]    bh = {w[7], w[5], w[3], w[1]};\n         wire [3:0]    bm = {w[6], w[4], w[2], w[0]};\n         wire [3:0]    bl = {w[5], w[3], w[1], 1'b0};\n         wire [3:0]    bone = bm ^ bl;                             // |d| == 1\n         wire [3:0]    btwo = (bh & ~bm & ~bl) | (~bh & bm & bl);  // |d| == 2\n         wire [3:0]    bneg = bh & ~(bm & bl);                     // d < 0\n         assign ctl_v[0][j] = {bneg[3], btwo[3], bone[3], bneg[2], btwo[2],\n                               bone[2], bneg[1], btwo[1], bone[1], bneg[0],\n                               btwo[0], bone[0]};\n\n         assign ps_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign pc_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column;\n         // recover the +131072 case:  V in [-130048, 131072] is unique mod\n         // 2^18, and only the top value 131072 has sum[16:10] zero with\n         // sum[17] set.\n         wire [ACCW-1:0] sum = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n         wire            sel = sum[ACCW-1] & (|sum[ACCW-2:ACCW-8]);\n         assign c_bot[j*(ACCW+1) +: (ACCW+1)] = {sel, sum};\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk       (clk),\n               .ld        (ld),\n               .en        (en),\n               .ctl_north (ctl_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west    (a_h[i][j]),\n               .na_west   (na_h[i][j]),\n               .ps_n      (ps_v[i][j]),\n               .pc_n      (pc_v[i][j]),\n               .ctl_south (ctl_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east    (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .na_east   (na_h[i][j+1]),\n               .ps_s      (ps_v[i+1][j]),\n               .pc_s      (pc_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input               clk,\n    input               rst,     // async active high, only before the stream\n    input               w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   w_data,\n    input               a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]   a_data,\n    output              c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   localparam ACW = 19;         // output accumulator width\n   localparam NAW = DW + 1;     // exact -A width\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zero fill when a_valid low.\n   // The negated activation -A is computed at the input and skewed in\n   // parallel so that every PE sees registered (A, -A) pairs.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0]   a_left;\n   wire [N*NAW-1:0]  na_left;\n   genvar            i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0]  din  = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         wire [NAW-1:0] dinn = ~{din[DW-1], din} + 1'b1;\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = din;\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = dinn;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [i*DW-1:0]  sr;\n            reg [i*NAW-1:0] srn;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en) begin\n                 sr  <= {sr[(i-1)*DW-1:0], din};\n                 srn <= {srn[(i-1)*NAW-1:0], dinn};\n              end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW]    = sr[i*DW-1 -: DW];\n            assign na_left[i*NAW +: NAW] = srn[i*NAW-1 -: NAW];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // the systolic array\n   wire [N*ACW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACW-1)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .na_left(na_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, then sign-extend to ACCW\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACW +: ACW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-ACW){cin[ACW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [D*ACW-1:0] dr;\n            always @(posedge clk)\n              if (en)\n                dr <= {dr[(D-1)*ACW-1:0], cin};\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-ACW){dr[D*ACW-1]}}, dr[D*ACW-1 -: ACW]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design14.v')
  -> run_evaluation(filename='design14.v')

  -> edit_file(filename='design14.v', new_string='         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column\n         // (parallel-prefix adder + recovery of the +131072 case).\n         wire [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n         wire            sel;\n         cadd18 u_cadd (.a(ps_v[N][j]), .b(pc_v[N][j]), .s(sum), .sel(sel));\n         assign c_bot[j*(ACCW+1) +: (ACCW+1)] = {sel, sum};', old_string='         // final carry-propagate conversion at the bottom of each column;\n         // recover the +131072 case:  V in [-130048, 131072] is unique mod\n         // 2^18, and only the top value 131072 has sum[16:10] zero with\n         // sum[17] set.\n         wire [ACCW-1:0] sum = ps_v[N][j] + pc_v[N][j];\n         wire            sel = sum[ACCW-1] & (|sum[ACCW-2:ACCW-8]);\n         assign c_bot[j*(ACCW+1) +: (ACCW+1)] = {sel, sum};')

[ASSISTANT]
Design14 confirmed: **ADP 1.977M** — 57% better than the 4.62M baseline, 100% correct (6782/6782 checks).

Here's a summary of the optimization journey and the final design:

## Final design (`design14.v`) — ADP 1.977M (area 4277, delay 462ps)

**Key optimizations over the original logikbench RTL:**

1. **Exact 18-bit carry-save accumulators** (vs 32-bit ripple adders): every partial sum holds ≤ 8 products of |p| ≤ 2^14, so V ∈ [−130048, +131072] — all values distinct mod 2^18! The partial sum descends each column as a redundant (sum, carry) pair merged by full-adder arrays (no carry chains); one carry-propagate adder per column converts it, with a single `sel` bit recovering the +131072 case (`sum[17] & |sum[16:10]`).

2. **Radix-4 Booth multiply fused into the accumulate**: the stationary weight is decoded **once at load** into 4 Booth digits `{one, two, neg}` (12-bit code shifts south — no raw weight register in the PEs at all). Each digit's row is `X = neg ? −A : A; row = (one&X | two&X<<1) << 2i` — exact, with **no +1 correction vectors**. Only 6 vectors feed a **3-level 3:2 compressor** (the Dadda minimum for this operand count).

3. **−A computed once per array row** at the activation input and registered through the input skew and the east-west chain alongside A, so every PE row generator is a pure AND-OR of register outputs.

4. **No datapath resets** (only the 15-bit valid pipeline is reset — the only output checked every cycle); all datapath state is self-defined before the first `c_valid`.

The design preserves the exact cycle-by-cycle contract: weight-load stalling, zero-injection on `a_valid` gaps, back-to-back tiles, mid-drain weight reloads, and the 15-cycle latency — all verified against the golden trace. The critical paths (compute row-gen→tree and column CPA) are balanced at ~462ps.

[ASSISTANT]
Final result confirmed. **`design14.v` — ADP 1.977M, 100% correct (6782/6782 checks)**, a **57% improvement** over the 4.62M baseline.

## Summary of the optimization path

| Design | Key idea | ADP |
|---|---|---|
| baseline | original logikbench RTL | 4.62M |
| design.v | exact 19-bit accumulators + no datapath resets | 3.22M |
| design2 | carry-save (sum,carry) descent, 1 CPA/column | 2.82M |
| design5/6/7 | radix-4 Booth multiply fused with accumulate | 2.64→2.53M |
| design8/10 | exact −A rows (kills +1 correction vector, 3-level tree) | 2.46→2.36M |
| design12 | Booth code shifts south — no weight register in PEs | 2.18M |
| design13 | 12-bit `{one,two,neg}` code + factored row-gen | 2.17M |
| **design14** | **18-bit accumulator (range [−130048,131072] is unique mod 2¹⁸) + `sel` recovery bit** | **1.977M** |

**Final architecture highlights:**
- **18-bit carry-save pair** per PE instead of 32-bit ripple adders — mathematically exact since every partial sum holds ≤8 products of |p|≤2¹⁴ and all reachable values are distinct modulo 2¹⁸; one `sel = sum[17] & |sum[16:10]` bit recovers the +131072 endpoint.
- **Booth-digit weight codes** decoded once at the array's top edge at load time; the 12-bit code (not the raw weight) shifts south, eliminating the per-PE weight register.
- **Exact rows** `row_i = (one&X | two&X<<1) << 2i` with `X = neg ? −A : A`, where −A is computed once per array row and registered alongside A — no correction vectors, so only 6 vectors enter a **3-level Dadda-minimal 3:2 compressor**.
- Cycle-accurate freeze semantics, zero-injection, back-to-back tiles and mid-drain reloads all preserved and verified against the golden trace.

## Architecture of the final design

1. **Exact 18-bit carry-save accumulation** — every partial sum holds ≤ 8 products of |p| ≤ 2¹⁴, so values lie in [−130048, +131072], all distinct modulo 2¹⁸. The partial sum descends each column as a redundant (sum, carry) pair merged by full-adder arrays; one carry-propagate adder per column converts it, with a single `sel = sum[17] & |sum[16:10]` bit recovering the +131072 endpoint.

2. **Radix-4 Booth multiply fused with the accumulate** — the stationary weight is decoded once at the array's top edge at load time into 4 digits stored as a 12-bit `{one, two, neg}` code that shifts south (no per-PE weight register). Rows are exact: `row_i = (one&X | two&X<<1) << 2i` with `X = neg ? −A : A`, where −A is computed once per array row and registered alongside A through the skew and east-west chains. No +1 correction vectors → only 6 vectors feed a **3-level, Dadda-minimal 3:2 compressor** with no carry chains.

3. **Minimal state** — no datapath resets (only the valid pipeline), enable-gated freeze preserving the exact w_valid stall semantics, zero-injection for a_valid gaps, and the exact 15-cycle output latency — all cycle-accurate to the original logikbench `tpu`.

## Summary

**Final result: `design14.v` — PASS, ADP 1.976e6** (area 4277, delay 462ps), down **57%** from the 4.62e6 baseline.

### 1. Approaches tried and what worked

| File | Idea | ADP |
|---|---|---|
| `starting_point.v` | original | 4.62M |
| `design.v` | 19-bit accumulators + no datapath resets | 3.22M |
| `design2.v` | carry-save (sum,carry) descent, 1 CPA/column | 2.82M |
| `design5-7.v` | radix-4 Booth multiply fused with accumulate | 2.64→2.53M |
| `design8/10.v` | exact −A rows → no +1 correction vector, 3-level tree | 2.46→2.36M |
| `design12/13.v` | Booth code (not raw weight) shifts south; 12-bit ctl; factored row-gen | 2.17M |
| **`design14.v`** | **18-bit accumulator + `sel` recovery bit** | **1.976M** |

### 2. Most impactful optimizations
- **Exact-width carry-save accumulators** replacing 32-bit ripple adders — biggest single win (3.2→2.8M), then further proof that only **18 bits** are needed (result range [−130048, +131072] is injective mod 2¹⁸; one `sel = sum[17]&|sum[16:10]` bit recovers +131072) — another −9%.
- **Fused Booth MAC**: exact rows `(one&X | two&X<<1)<<2i` with `X = neg?−A:A` eliminated the +1 correction vector, enabling a **3-level tree** (Dadda minimum for 6 vectors) instead of 4-5 levels.
- **−A precomputed once per array row** and registered alongside A (na_west/na_east path) — removed incrementers from all PE critical paths.
- **Weight decoded at load** into a 12-bit Booth code that shifts south — deleted the per-PE weight register.

### 3. Regressions / dead ends
- Fully fused 11-operand tree (`design3`): faster but area exploded (5.1M).
- Verilog width trap (`c1 & a19` zero-extends instead of replicating) — broke `design7` until `{ACCW{...}}` was used.
- Kogge-Stone bottom adder and "sel at output" variants degraded abc's *global* mapping (2.15M, 2.02M) — the balanced ripple version was better.
- Unshifted-carry pair (`value = s+2c`) broke FA weight assumptions — failed sim; reverted.
- Flat AND-OR row-gen, swapped tree groupings, OR-after-shift rows — all slightly worse.
- `target_delay` sweeps were mostly inert (best: 480 ≈ default 500).

### 4. Lessons
- **Value-range proofs enable real width cuts** — the 18-bit accumulator insight was the largest single gain after CSA.
- **Signed negation tricks matter**: `−A` as a registered operand beats `~A+1` corrections scattered into compressor columns (the correction budget at low columns is provably insufficient: 82 < 85).
- **abc optimizes the flattened AIG globally** — "faster" hand structures (prefix adders) can backfire; measure every variant.
- Early bug (`1-bit & vector` zero-extension) cost a cycle; always use explicit replication masks in structural RTL.
